```
Author: Jordan Farr <jfjordanfarr@gmail.com>
Date:   Thu Mar 26 20:14:40 2026 +0000

    feat(membrane-map): FLIP animations, cross-column directory bands, and hover dimming

    Membrane Map pin-active view gains smooth visual transitions and
    hierarchical directory membranes that span across dependency-flow columns.

    Implementation:
    - Extract animation.ts: FLIP position+scale animations with Promise-based
      timing, SVG line-draw stroke-dashoffset effects, fade-in for new elements
    - Cross-column directory bands: trie-based hierarchical computation that
      groups files by directory (Strategy B+C hybrid), collapses single-child
      chains, and renders nested CSS Grid membranes with clickable labels
    - Bare-band rendering for root-level files (LCA directory) — no misleading
      membrane border when files aren't in a subdirectory
    - Selection-only fast path via structural fingerprint to skip full DOM
      rebuilds on same-structure click changes
    - Hover dimming: event delegation on symbol rows adds/removes CSS class
      on SVG overlay for connection highlighting
    - Deferred connection drawing: SVG connectors wait for FLIP Promise to
      resolve so anchor measurements are at final positions

    Tests:
    - 41 pin-layout tests covering band computation, hierarchy, trie
      collapse, interval scheduling, root-level files, and LCA integration
    - 867 tests passing across 67 files

    Documentation:
    - Authored animation.ts Live Doc with creation provenance
    - Live Docs regenerated for all modified membraneView modules
```

## Blind Spot Analysis

**Today's chat had 1 compaction**, after reading raw 3/26.1 lines 1200–2400. This meant the summary file was written with L1–2400 known only via compaction-compressed notes, and L2400–3197 from direct reading. Targeted re-reads (L1-36, L102-165, L210-270, L355-520, L1130-1200) covered user prompts but left Copilot's mid-turn responses and compaction markers in the blind zone.

## Errors Found and Fixed (9 corrections)

| #   | Error                                                | Root Cause                                            | Fix                                                                                   |
| --- | ---------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 1   | Key Themes: "4 derailments"                          | Compaction count confused with derailment count       | → **3 derailments**                                                                   |
| 2   | Context: "four consecutive compaction events derail" | Same confusion                                        | → **three derailment-causing compactions** (L93, L147, L197) + 6 non-derailing        |
| 3   | Context: "corrective prompts three times"            | Omitted Turn 3 laziness correction                    | → **four corrective prompts** (3 derailments + 1 laziness)                            |
| 4   | Turn 2: "A second compaction occurs"                 | No compaction marker between L102-L121                | → **No compaction in this turn**; PDJ bookend reading is lazy, not compaction-induced |
| 5   | Turn 3: "creates…SUMMARIZED.md (Turns 1–4)"          | File creation happened in Turn 4, not 3               | → Turn 3 has the **second compaction** (L147), derails before producing output        |
| 6   | Turn 3: "A third compaction"                         | Numbering wrong                                       | → **second** compaction                                                               |
| 7   | Turn 4: "Loses context…begins debugging" only        | Missed that Copilot DID create the summary file       | → **successfully creates** the file before the **third compaction** (L197) derails    |
| 8   | Turn 4 title: "third derailment"                     | Should be second (Turn 2=1st, Turn 4=2nd, Turn 5=3rd) | → **second derailment**                                                               |
| 9   | Session Statistics: "~6+ (4 setup + 2+ impl)"        | Undercounted                                          | → **9 total** (6 setup/spot-check + 3 implementation); 3 caused derailments           |

Completed: _Propose solutions for sizing bug_ (4/4)

## Bug Verified: Mixed-Content Directory File Sizing

### Root Cause

In layout.ts, children are weighted for the squarified treemap:

- **Directories**: `weight = computeWeight(childDir)` — recursive total file count (e.g. 152)
- **Files**: `weight = 1` — fixed

The squarify algorithm at squarify.ts allocates area **strictly proportional to weight**. In mixed directories, files are crushed:

| Directory                 | Example: integration |
| ------------------------- | -------------------- |
| benchmarks (weight 152)   | 724x609 px           |
| fixtures (weight 40)      | 252x460 px           |
| tsconfig.json (weight 1)  | **68x43 px**         |
| clean-dist.mjs (weight 1) | **68x43 px**         |

Files get ~1% of the area. At 68x43, they can't display symbols (threshold: 120x100) and pins are unreachable.

### Why It Doesn't Affect Leaf-Only Directories

In browse-renderer.ts, when the focused directory contains **only files**, a special `isLeafDirectory` branch triggers card-grid rendering (Local-Map-style cards with full symbol pins). Mixed directories fall through to the normal treemap path where files are positioned `membrane-leaf` tiles at their tiny computed rects.

---

### Proposed Solutions

#### Solution A: Minimum File Weight Floor (simplest)

In `layoutDirectory`, boost file weights in mixed directories so they always occupy a minimum visible fraction:

```typescript
// After collecting childItems in layout.ts:
const hasDirs = childItems.some((c) => c.kind === "dir");
const hasFiles = childItems.some((c) => c.kind === "file");
if (hasDirs && hasFiles) {
  const avgDirWeight =
    childItems.filter((c) => c.kind === "dir").reduce((s, c) => s + c.weight, 0) /
    childItems.filter((c) => c.kind === "dir").length;
  const minFileWeight = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(avgDirWeight * 0.15));
  for (const item of childItems) {
    if (item.kind === "file") item.weight = minFileWeight;
  }
}
```

**Pros**: ~10 lines, localized, files become visible immediately.  
**Cons**: Distorts "true" treemap proportionality.

#### Solution B: Split Layout (files shelf + directory treemap)

Partition the content rect into two zones — e.g. give files a guaranteed 15-25% vertical strip at the bottom, lay out directories in the remaining space:

```
┌──────────────────────────────────┐
│  Directory Treemap  (75-85%)     │
│  ┌────────┐ ┌──────────────────┐ │
│  │ dir-a  │ │     dir-b        │ │
│  └────────┘ └──────────────────┘ │
├──────────────────────────────────┤
│  Files Shelf (15-25%)            │
│  ┌─────┐ ┌─────┐ ┌─────┐        │
│  │ a.ts│ │ b.ts│ │ c.ts│        │
│  └─────┘ └─────┘ └─────┘        │
└──────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Pros**: Clean separation, files always usable, directory proportions stay accurate.  
**Cons**: Breaks pure treemap metaphor, more layout code.

#### Solution C: Extend Card-Grid to Mixed Directories (recommended)

When the focused directory has files mixed with subdirectories, render it as a **hybrid**: collapsed directory tiles at the top (treemap-sized), file cards below (card-grid). This extends the existing `isLeafDirectory` rendering path:

```typescript
// In browse-renderer.ts, change isLeafDirectory to isFileContentDirectory:
const hasFiles = node.children.some((c) => !c.isDirectory);
const hasDirs = node.children.some((c) => c.isDirectory);
const isFileContentDirectory = node.id === focusedDirectory && hasFiles;

if (isFileContentDirectory && hasDirs) {
  // Render subdirectories as collapsed tiles in a top section
  // Render files as card-grid below (existing card rendering)
} else if (isFileContentDirectory) {
  // Pure leaf — existing card-grid path
}
```

**Pros**: Files always get card-grid treatment with full symbol pins. Best UX.  
**Cons**: Most complex to implement. Need to handle the directory/card split carefully.

#### Solution D: Logarithmic Directory Weight (least disruption)

Replace `weight: computeWeight(childDir)` with `weight: 1 + Math.log2(computeWeight(childDir))`:

- File: weight 1
- Directory with 152 files: weight ~8.2 (instead of 152)
- Result: files get ~11% (instead of ~1%)

**Pros**: Single-line change. Preserves ordering.  
**Cons**: Distorts visual meaning — a 152-file dir looks similar to a 10-file dir.

---

### Recommendation

**Solution C** is the "right way" — it guarantees files are always interactable via card-grid rendering regardless of sibling directory sizes. **Solution A** should be applied as a safety floor even with Solution C, so that files in non-focused mixed directories (visible as tiles) are never invisibly small.

Which direction would you like to pursue?

User: Please pursue Solution C. }

Completed: _Build, test, and rebuild Explorer bundle_ (5/6)

**Solution C is implemented and verified.** Here's the summary:

### What Changed

**3 files modified, 3 tests added:**

1. layout.ts — When the focused directory (innermost on the focus path) contains both subdirectories and files, files are excluded from `computeSquarifiedLayout`. Directory tiles get 100% of the treemap area. File children still get `MembraneNode` entries with zero-area rects.

2. browse-renderer.ts — New `isMixedDirectory` branch between the existing `isLeafDirectory` and normal treemap paths. Creates a two-zone DOM: treemap `membrane__content` div for directory tiles + `membrane__card-grid--hybrid` div for file cards with full symbol pins.

3. membrane.css — Added `.membrane__card-grid--hybrid` for visual separation between zones.

4. layout.test.ts — 3 new tests: mixed-content excludes files, non-focus unaffected, pure-leaf unaffected.

### Visual Verification

Navigated to live-docs (21 files + 3 subdirs):

- **Before**: Files at ~1% area due to weight=1 vs directories with weight=N — impossible to pin symbols
- **After**: Directory tiles (ADAPTERS, HEURISTICS, ANALYSIS) fill the treemap; 17 file cards render below as full card-grid cards with visible symbol pins — identical to the `isLeafDirectory` card-grid UX

The card grid extends below the treemap zone, consistent with the existing pan/zoom paradigm used by `isLeafDirectory`. All 870 tests pass.

Made changes.

User: This is quite impressive! I really like this. Let's now make one more enhancement that I think is going to ensure that the layout is even cleaner and the transition from the broader map into the more local map-like (cross-membrane traversing) view of symbol-to-symbol dependency flows much more understandable and obvious:

1. All nodes should be "collapsed" by default when no symbol pinning has occurred. As an example of what "collapsed" might look like, please see this screenshot of the Local Map of `symbolExtraction.ts` with only its `Internals` pseudo-symbol pinned. For all symbols that do not participate in interactions with the pinned `Internals` pseudo-symbol, they are not rendered. Thus, nodes that flank the focused node are roughly-equivalent in size unless they have specific participants that engage with that symbol. The view that we see of these "fully collapsed" nodes is what I would think of as the default view of any node once entering a directory that has leaf nodes.
2. The first click on a leaf node should pin _all_ of its symbols, leading to a simple transition to an equivalent of what the existing "Local Map" does, but with the added enhancement of seeing the parent "membranes" around the interactors.
3. (Optional, minor for now): Attempt to better-match the styling of "membranes" between the standard Membrane Map view (before any symbol pinning) and the symbol pinning view to ensure that the "common membranes" concept really sinks in as interpretable and understandable. It's okay if we want to diverge away from the very light blue that stacks on the Membrane Map (and Circuit Board) canonically. Better readability is certainly preferable, and I think the Local Map and local map-like view brought about by symbol pinning in the Membrane Map is easier to read directory names on.

User: >But re-examining the Local Map screenshot the user referenced, I'm noticing the non-participating nodes still display their full structure—filename, code path, all symbols with dots, internals. The "collapsed" visual effect comes from the absence of connection lines, not from hiding content.

Not so. The symbols on nodes in other columns which do not participate in interactions with `Internals` pseudo-symbol on the selected artifact really are not rendered. The effect is subtle if you see so many neighbors without listed public symbols on them (and they're dim, which can make it a little harder to tell by screenshot as well I suppose -- the dimming would obviously not be necessary when looking at a directory of these "collapsed" leaf nodes). But it is monumentally space-saving. I can tell you that the layout in the local map shifts _dramatically_ when I pin that "Internals" pseudo-symbol (to my chigrin; I have to pan around and find the focused artifact again)

The only other thing I think I didn't consider when I asked you the above was how to handle _subsequent_ clicks once the first node has had all of its symbols activated. I would be open to a single click on a node un-collapsing its symbols but _not yet_ causing pinning and connection stuff to happen. What I really want is some obvious and easy way to pin all symbols on a leaf node, and I want it to be idiomatic and simple without requiring a bunch of new UI (unless we feel it's necessary to have a "pin all" checkbox or something). It would be nice if the act of opening the detail panel for a node (single click on node card away from symbols if uncollapsed) was independent of the act of pinning all of its symbols.

GitHub Copilot:

Thank you for the correction. The "collapsing" in the Local Map is actual symbol row omission, not just dimming — and it's dramatically space-saving. Let me re-plan with that understanding:

**Flow:**

1. Cards start collapsed (name, path, internals anchor only — no individual symbol rows)
2. Single click on collapsed card → expand (reveal symbols) + select (detail panel)
3. Single click on expanded card body → select only (detail panel)
4. Click on individual symbols → toggle pin (unchanged)
5. Double-click on expanded card (or "📌" button) → pin all symbols

1. **Cards collapsed by default** — file cards in leaf directories now show only the filename, path, symbol count summary (e.g. "12 symbols"), and an Internals anchor. Dramatically reduces visual noise and card height.

2. **Click-to-expand + separate pin-all** — Single click on a collapsed card expands it (reveals all symbol rows with pin anchors) and selects it (detail panel). The 📌 pin-all button in the header or double-click on an expanded card pins all symbols. Expanding and pinning are independent actions.

3. **Membrane styling improvement** — Added subtle blue `background` tints at each depth level (`rgba(0, 20, 50, 0.25/0.18/0.12)`) and brightened label color to `rgba(180, 210, 240, 0.7)` for better readability of directory labels against the dark background.

**Files changed:**

- browse-renderer.ts — `BrowseRenderCallbacks` interface (2 new callbacks), `renderBrowseMode` and `renderNode` (expandedCards parameter), `renderFileCard` rewritten with collapsed/expanded dual-mode
- index.ts — `expandedCards` state, `structuralKey` fingerprint, `onExpandCard`/`onPinAllSymbols` callbacks
- membrane.css — Membrane depth backgrounds, label color, collapsed card styles, header-row layout, pin-all button, symbol-summary

**Test results:** 870/870 passing.

User: I think this is very cool, but I don't love the pin-emoji button. Emoji in UI has rapidly fallen out of fashion as a telltale sign of "AI Slop". You'll see that we still have some, but we should make every effort to minimize that. Nevertheless, this is rapidly improving. Can we now get the Membrane Map to now respect the "test-backed" styling for files which are backed by a test, stop showing only the "Internals" pesudo-symbol in "collapsed" leaf nodes (gives false impression that the node has no symbols), and get the "Tuning Panel"'s settings working on both the Local Map and the Membrane Map (specifically the settings under "Bezier Curve" and "Local Map". The "Click Behavior" and "Visual" checkboxes have all lost their functionality altogether and should be safe to remove).

Given the user says they've "lost their functionality," and these were originally built for earlier views, it might be that the views they affect are unused.

**Bezier Curve**: These sliders update `state.tuning.bezier.*` and call `drawLocalConnections()` which redraws Local Map SVG connections. The Membrane Map's `drawConnections` (in focal-overlay.ts) uses `routeConnection` which defaults to `DEFAULT_BEZIER_TUNING` and doesn't receive state tuning.

**Local Map settings**: Sliders update CSS custom properties on `.local-layout` — these work for Local Map only. The Membrane Map doesn't use these CSS properties.

   - The **opposite endpoint** of that connection gets the `.membrane-focal-pin--participating` + `.membrane-row--participating` classes

Completed: _Rebuild and verify fix_ (7/7)

**Fix complete.** The column gap slider now affects the Membrane Map's pin-active layout. Here's what was wrong and what I changed:

**Root cause:** Two issues conspiring together:

1. `.pa-grid-container` in membrane.css had a hardcoded `gap: 12px 32px` — it never read any CSS variable
2. `wireLocalMapSlider` in tuning.ts only set `--local-column-gap` on `.local-layout` (the Local Map container), which doesn't exist when the Membrane Map is active

**Fix (3 edits across 2 files):**

1. **CSS**: Changed `.pa-grid-container { gap: 12px 32px }` → `gap: 12px var(--local-column-gap, 32px)` — now reads the CSS variable with 32px fallback
2. **JS (event handler)**: `wireLocalMapSlider` now sets CSS properties on `document.documentElement` instead of only `.local-layout`, so the variable cascades to both views
3. **JS (initial sync)**: `syncTuningControlsFromState` also sets CSS properties on `document.documentElement` instead of only `.local-layout`, so the initial/persisted value is applied on page load

**Playwright proof:** Column gap goes from 160px → 24px when slider is moved from 160 → 24 (previously stayed stuck at 32px).

Made changes.

User: See attached 3 screenshots which demonstrate the progression of the view as the column gap is slid from max to min value. Notice that connectors do not shift and soon float off. Additionally, column resizing appears to resize the node cards themselves. Finally, you'll see that the prior request for pins which are participating in an interaction to light up was not fulfilled (indication that the symbol has an inbound or outbound connection but is not actively "pinned" (feel free to check the symbol pinning styling in the local map to see what it should look like when a formal "pin" has happened on a symbol -- there should be an entire border around pinned symbols)).

Starting: _Fix connectors not redrawing on gap change_ (2/6)

Let me now trace exactly what happens when `onRender()` is called from the slider. Let me look at `renderCurrentView` and how it calls the membrane render:

Searched for text `renderCurrentView` (`**/packages/scripts/src/live-docs/explorer/client/index.ts`), 9 results

Read [](file:///workspaces/Live-Documentation/packages/scripts/src/live-docs/explorer/client/index.ts#876-876), lines 876 to 910

Read [](file:///workspaces/Live-Documentation/packages/scripts/src/live-docs/explorer/client/views/membraneView/index.ts#115-115), lines 115 to 130

Now I see the core problem. The `structuralKey()` doesn't include any tuning values. When the slider changes:

1. `wireLocalMapSlider` calls `onRender()` → `renderCurrentView()` → `membraneView.render()`
2. `render()` calls `trySelectionOnlyUpdate()` which compares `structuralKey()` — but the key hasn't changed (same pins, same directory, same cards)
3. Fast path is taken — no DOM rebuild, no connection redraw
4. The CSS variable changes the gap, but SVG connections still reference old positions

**Fix for Issue 1 (connectors not shifting):** The structural key needs to include tuning values that affect layout, or better yet, when the column gap changes we need to force a connection redraw. The simplest fix: after the CSS variable changes, schedule a connection redraw (measure new positions and redraw SVG). We can't just include tuning in the structural key because that would force a full DOM rebuild (expensive) when all we need is to re-measure anchor positions and redraw paths.

**Fix for Issue 2 (card width):** Change `minmax(220px, 320px)` to a fixed width like `280px` or use `clamp()` so cards maintain consistent width.

**Fix for Issue 3 (participating indication):** The hover-based participating works but the user wants **always-on** indication. In pin-active mode, symbol rows that have visible connections should be styled differently from those that don't.

The `.pa-band-inner` is a NESTED grid with its own column definitions. Cards are inside `pa-band-slot` elements within `pa-band-inner`. Let me check what column template `pa-band-inner` uses:

Searched for regex `pa-band-inner|gridTemplateColumns` (`**/pin-active-renderer.ts`), 5 results

Found it! Line 214 and 234: `innerGrid.style.gridTemplateColumns = `repeat(${bandSpan}, 1fr)``

The inner grid uses `1fr` for its columns, which means they share available space equally. When the outer gap changes, the available space in a spanning band changes, so the inner columns (and thus cards) resize. I also need to fix the inner grid's `gap` to use the column gap CSS variable. The `1fr` in the inner grid is the cause. When the outer grid gap changes, the spanning band's width changes, and `1fr` redistributes to fill. The fix: instead of `1fr`, use a fixed width that matches the outer grid columns. Since the outer grid uses `320px`, the inner grid should too:

Replacing 5 lines with 5 lines in [](file:///workspaces/Live-Documentation/packages/scripts/src/live-docs/explorer/client/views/membraneView/pin-active-renderer.ts)


`redrawConnections()` IS being called with `hasSvg: true, anchorCount: 226, connCount: 37`. So the function runs, `drawConnections` is called... but the SVG paths don't update.

Wait — the anchors store references to DOM elements from the _initial_ render. If the DOM was completely replaced during a subsequent render, the old element references would be detached. But in this case, the fast-path was taken (no DOM rebuild), so the elements should still be in the DOM.

Completed: _Final Playwright verification_ (3/3)

All three issues are resolved and verified:

**Issue 1 — Connectors don't shift with gap:** Fixed by adding `redrawConnections()` to `MembraneViewApi`, wiring it through the tuning panel slider handler. When the column-gap slider changes, `drawConnections()` re-measures anchor positions via `getBoundingClientRect()` and redraws all SVG paths. Verified: first path x-coordinate correctly shifts from 703→759→839 as gap goes from 24→80→160.

**Issue 2 — Column resizing changes card width:** Fixed by changing inner grid `gridTemplateColumns` from `repeat(N, 1fr)` to `repeat(N, 320px)` in pin-active-renderer.ts. Cards remain 304px wide at all gap values.

**Issue 3 — Participating endpoints not indicated:** Fixed by adding `markConnectedEndpoints()` in focal-overlay.ts — scans SVG connections, applies `.membrane-card__symbol-row--connected` class to unpinned endpoints and `.membrane-card__symbol-row--pinned` class to pinned symbols. CSS provides a green left-border highlight for connected rows and a blue box-shadow border for pinned rows.

All 870 tests pass. Debug logging has been removed.

Here's the proposed commit message:

---

```
feat(membrane-map): hybrid mixed-content layout, collapsed cards, connection redraw, and endpoint indicators

Membrane Map receives five major enhancements addressing layout, interactivity,
and visual feedback in the pin-active dependency-flow view.

Mixed-content directory layout (Solution C):
- Directories containing both files and subdirectories now render a hybrid
  two-zone layout: directories get squarified treemap tiles, files render
  as a card grid underneath
- Files excluded from squarified layout in focused mixed-content directories,
  solving the area-crushing problem (weight=1 files dwarfed by weight=N dirs)
- 3 new layout tests verify mixed-content, non-focus, and pure-leaf behavior

Collapsed cards with click-to-expand:
- File cards default to collapsed state showing name, path, and symbol count
- Click expands to reveal all symbol rows with pin anchors
- Pin-all button (CSS icon, not emoji) bulk-pins all symbols in a card
- Test-backed cards receive golden border/glow via .membrane-card--test-backed

Pin-active connection improvements:
- redrawConnections() API for lightweight SVG connection refresh without
  full FLIP animation — reuses stored anchor references to re-measure
  positions via getBoundingClientRect
- Column gap slider now propagates to Membrane Map: CSS variable set on
  document.documentElement, grid containers read var(--local-column-gap)
- Fixed-width 320px grid columns prevent card width distortion during
  gap adjustment (was minmax/1fr)
- Bezier tuning parameters forwarded to drawConnections for live control

Endpoint indication:
- markConnectedEndpoints() scans SVG data attributes to apply
  .membrane-card__symbol-row--connected class to unpinned endpoint rows
  (green left-border) with case-insensitive symbol matching
- Pinned symbol rows receive .membrane-card__symbol-row--pinned class
  (blue box-shadow border matching Local Map convention)
- Hover dimming guards against no-op activation when symbol has zero
  connections; participating endpoints marked during hover

Dead code removal:
- Click Behavior and Visual tuning sections removed from template.html,
  tuning.ts, types.ts, and local-storage.ts
- showTypeBadges hardcoded to true in card-factory.ts

Documentation:
- Dev Day 82 summary created (2026-03-26.1.SUMMARIZED.md) with 9
  compaction-induced blind spot corrections
- 11 Live Doc authored sections updated with Dev Day 83 provenance
- Census files updated (3 intent signals, UC-094 bracket note)

Tests: 870 unit (67 files) + 28 integration, all passing
Files: 28 changed, +763 -311

Refs: Dev Day 83 (2026-03-27)
```

Starting: _Verbalize loose ends_ (7/7)

**Step 7 — Loose ends:**

1. **Membrane Map is not yet the default view** — users still land on Knowledge Sources. Making Membrane Map the default (or at least a persisted preference) is a future consideration.

2. **No integration test for the visual enhancements** — all Playwright verification was manual. The hybrid layout, collapsed cards, and connection redraw changes are covered by 3 unit tests for the layout math, but DOM-level behavior (expand/collapse, pin-all, connection redraw on slider change) lacks automated Playwright/E2E coverage.

3. **Detail panel intercepts clicks** — throughout Playwright testing, the Knowledge Sources detail panel repeatedly intercepted clicks meant for the Membrane Map. This is a known UX friction point that should be addressed (auto-close on view switch, z-index fix, or similar).

4. **`regenerate-benchmarks.ts` exceeds 1000 lines** (1202) — flagged by tech-debt detection. Unrelated to this commit but worth noting for future refactoring.

5. **Pin-all does not unpin** — the pin-all button only adds pins; there's no "unpin-all" counterpart. The user hasn't requested this yet but it's an obvious UX gap.

6. **Membrane Map URL state doesn't include `expandedCards`** — if a user shares a URL with expanded cards, the recipient will see them collapsed. This is a minor gap since card expansion is a transient interaction.

Completed: _Verbalize loose ends_ (7/7)
